    67 | function resolveFanmTeamIdentity(team = {}) {
    68 |   if (team?.teamIdentity?.abbr) {
    69 |     return team.teamIdentity;
    70 |   }
    71 | 
    72 |   const candidates = [
    73 |     team?.abbr,
    74 |     team?.abbrev,
    75 |     team?.label,
    76 |     team?.name,
    77 |     team?.title,
    78 |     team?.teamName,
    79 |     team?.teamIdentity?.abbr,
    80 |     team?.teamIdentity?.name,
    81 |   ]
    82 |     .map(normalizeTeamLibraryKey)
    83 |     .filter(Boolean);
    84 | 
    85 |   return (
    86 |     FANM_TEAM_LIBRARY.find((entry) => {
    87 |       const libraryKeys = [
    88 |         entry?.abbr,
    89 |         entry?.name,
    90 |       ]
    91 |         .map(normalizeTeamLibraryKey)
    92 |         .filter(Boolean);
    93 | 
    94 |       return candidates.some((candidate) =>
    95 |         libraryKeys.includes(candidate)
    96 |       );
    97 |     }) || team?.teamIdentity || null
    98 |   );
    99 | }

  1065 | async function appendEventToFirestore(
  1066 |   event,
  1067 |   summaryInfo,
  1068 |   secondsLeft,
  1069 |   matchSeconds
  1070 | ) {
  1071 |   try {
  1072 |     const ref = getMatchDoc(db, MATCH_DOC_ID);
  1073 | 
  1074 |     const common = {
  1075 |       ...summaryInfo,
  1076 |       matchMode: summaryInfo.matchMode || "5_V_5",
  1077 |       gameFormat: summaryInfo.gameFormat || "5_V_5",
  1078 |       playersPerSide: summaryInfo.playersPerSide || 5,
  1079 |       matchSeconds: matchSeconds ?? 0,
  1080 |       secondsLeft:
  1081 |         typeof secondsLeft === "number" ? Math.max(secondsLeft, 0) : null,
  1082 |       isFinished: false,
  1083 |       lastUpdated: serverTimestamp(),
  1084 |     };
  1085 | 
  1086 |     try {
  1087 |       await updateDoc(ref, {
  1088 |         events: arrayUnion(event),
  1089 |         ...common,
  1090 |       });
  1091 |     } catch (_) {
  1092 |       await setDoc(
  1093 |         ref,
  1094 |         {
  1095 |           events: [event],
  1096 |           createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
  1097 |           ...common,
  1098 |         },
  1099 |         { merge: true }
  1100 |       );
  1101 |     }
  1102 |   } catch (err) {
  1103 |     console.error("⚠️ Failed to mirror event to Firestore:", err);
  1104 |   }
  1105 | }

  1924 | function getLocalDateKey(dateInput = new Date()) {
  1925 |   const d = dateInput instanceof Date ? dateInput : new Date(dateInput || Date.now());
  1926 |   const safe = Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? new Date() : d;
  1927 |   const year = safe.getFullYear();
  1928 |   const month = String(safe.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  1929 |   const day = String(safe.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
  1930 |   return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
  1931 | }
  1932 | 
  1933 | function normalizeFormatKey(gameFormat = "5_V_5") {
  1934 |   const raw = String(gameFormat || "5_V_5").trim().toLowerCase();
  1935 |   if (raw.includes("7")) return "7v7";
  1936 |   if (raw.includes("6")) return "6v6";
  1937 |   return "5v5";
  1938 | }
  1939 |

  2715 |     async function requestScreenWakeLock() {
  2716 |       try {
  2717 |         if (!("wakeLock" in navigator)) return;
  2718 |         if (wakeLockRef.current) return;
  2719 | 
  2720 |         const lock = await navigator.wakeLock.request("screen");
  2721 |         if (!mounted) {
  2722 |           lock.release?.().catch(() => {});
  2723 |           return;
  2724 |         }
  2725 | 
  2726 |         wakeLockRef.current = lock;
  2727 | 
  2728 |         lock.addEventListener?.("release", () => {
  2729 |           if (mounted) wakeLockRef.current = null;
  2730 |         });
  2731 |       } catch (err) {
  2732 |         // Some browsers/devices do not support wake lock. Silent fallback is fine.
  2733 |         console.warn("Screen wake lock unavailable:", err);
  2734 |       }
  2735 |     }

  4015 |   const addAdditionalTime = (seconds) => {

    12 | // same helper used in LiveMatchPage (reimplemented here)
    13 | function formatSeconds(s) {
    14 |   const v = typeof s === "number" && !Number.isNaN(s) ? s : 0;
    15 |   const m = Math.floor(v / 60)
    16 |     .toString()
    17 |     .padStart(2, "0");
    18 |   const sec = (v % 60).toString().padStart(2, "0");
    19 |   return `${m}:${sec}`;
    20 | }
    21 | 
    22 | // Compact label for mobile
    23 | function getShortName(label) {
    24 |   if (!label) return "";
    25 |   const map = {
    26 |     
    27 |     Madrid: "MAD",
    28 |     
    29 |   };
    30 |   if (map[label]) return map[label];
    31 | 
    32 |   const cleaned = label.replace(/team/gi, "").trim();
    33 |   if (!cleaned) return label;
    34 |   return cleaned.slice(0, 3).toUpperCase();
    35 | }
    36 | 
    37 | function normalizeIdentityLookup(value = "") {
    38 |   return String(value || "")
    39 |     .trim()
    40 |     .toLowerCase()
    41 |     .replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "");
    42 | }
    43 | 
    44 | const FANM_TEAM_IDENTITY_LOOKUP = [
    45 |   ...(Array.isArray(FANM_NATIONAL_TEAMS) ? FANM_NATIONAL_TEAMS : []),
    46 |   ...(Array.isArray(FANM_PRO_CLUBS) ? FANM_PRO_CLUBS : []),
    47 | ];
    48 | 
    49 | function resolveSpectatorTeamIdentity(team = {}) {
    50 |   if (team?.teamIdentity) return team.teamIdentity;
    51 | 
    52 |   const keys = [
    53 |     team?.abbr,
    54 |     team?.label,
    55 |     team?.name,
    56 |     team?.title,
    57 |   ]
    58 |     .map(normalizeIdentityLookup)
    59 |     .filter(Boolean);
    60 | 
    61 |   return (
    62 |     FANM_TEAM_IDENTITY_LOOKUP.find((identity) => {
    63 |       const identityKeys = [
    64 |         identity?.abbr,
    65 |         identity?.name,
    66 |       ]
    67 |         .map(normalizeIdentityLookup)
    68 |         .filter(Boolean);
    69 | 
    70 |       return keys.some((key) => identityKeys.includes(key));
    71 |     }) || null
    72 |   );
    73 | }
    74 | 
    75 | function getSpectatorTeamLabel(team = {}, short = false) {
    76 |   const identity = resolveSpectatorTeamIdentity(team);
    77 |   if (short && identity?.abbr) return identity.abbr;
    78 |   if (identity?.name) return identity.name;
    79 |   if (short) return team?.abbrev || getShortName(team?.label);
    80 |   return team?.label || team?.name || team?.title || "Team";
    81 | }
    82 | 
    83 | function SpectatorTeamBadge({ team, short = false }) {
    84 |   const identity = resolveSpectatorTeamIdentity(team);
    85 |   const label = getSpectatorTeamLabel(team, short);
    86 | 
    87 |   return (
    88 |     <span className="fanm-live-team-badge spectator-team-badge">
    89 |       {identity?.type === "national" && identity.flag ? (
    90 |         <span className="fanm-live-team-flag">{identity.flag}</span>
    91 |       ) : identity?.logo32 ? (
    92 |         <img
    93 |           src={identity.logo32}
    94 |           alt=""
    95 |           className="fanm-live-team-logo"
    96 |           onError={(event) => {
    97 |             event.currentTarget.style.display = "none";
    98 |           }}
    99 |         />
   100 |       ) : null}
   101 |       <span>{label}</span>
   102 |     </span>
   103 |   );
   104 | }

   107 | function getSpectatorTeamAccent(seed = "") {
   108 |   const palette = [
   109 |     {
   110 |       soft: "rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.14)",
   111 |       border: "rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.42)",
   112 |       dot: "#22c55e",
   113 |     },
   114 |     {
   115 |       soft: "rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.14)",
   116 |       border: "rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.42)",
   117 |       dot: "#3b82f6",
   118 |     },
   119 |     {
   120 |       soft: "rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.14)",
   121 |       border: "rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.42)",
   122 |       dot: "#ef4444",
   123 |     },
   124 |     {
   125 |       soft: "rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.14)",
   126 |       border: "rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.42)",
   127 |       dot: "#a855f7",
   128 |     },
   129 |     {
   130 |       soft: "rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.13)",
   131 |       border: "rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.4)",
   132 |       dot: "#facc15",
   133 |     },
   134 |   ];
   135 | 
   136 |   const key = String(seed || "");
   137 |   const total = key.split("").reduce((sum, ch) => sum + ch.charCodeAt(0), 0);
   138 |   return palette[total % palette.length];
   139 | }

   141 | function displaySpectatorName(name) {
   142 |   const clean = String(name || "").trim();
   143 |   if (!clean) return "Unknown player";
   144 |   return clean;
   145 | }

   147 | export function SpectatorPage(props) {
   148 |   // support either prop name to be safe with your existing App.jsx
   149 |   const goBack = props.onBackToLanding || props.onBack || (() => {});
   150 | 
   151 |   const [matchDoc, setMatchDoc] = useState(null);
   152 |   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   153 |   const [errorText, setErrorText] = useState("");
   154 | 
   155 |   // local countdown state for smoother timer
   156 |   const [localSecondsLeft, setLocalSecondsLeft] = useState(null);
   157 | 
   158 |   useEffect(() => {
   159 |     const ref = getMatchDoc(db, "current");
   160 | 
   161 |     const unsub = onSnapshot(
   162 |       ref,
   163 |       (snap) => {
   164 |         if (snap.exists()) {
   165 |           const data = snap.data();
   166 |           setMatchDoc(data);
   167 | 
   168 |           // sync local timer with server secondsLeft if available
   169 |           if (
   170 |             typeof data.secondsLeft === "number" &&
   171 |             Number.isFinite(data.secondsLeft)
   172 |           ) {
   173 |             setLocalSecondsLeft(Math.max(data.secondsLeft, 0));
   174 |           } else {
   175 |             setLocalSecondsLeft(null);
   176 |           }
   177 |         } else {
   178 |           setMatchDoc(null);
   179 |           setLocalSecondsLeft(null);
   180 |         }
   181 |         setLoading(false);
   182 |       },
   183 |       (err) => {
   184 |         console.error("Spectator onSnapshot error:", err);
   185 |         setErrorText("Could not connect to live match data.");
   186 |         setLoading(false);
   187 |       }
   188 |     );
   189 | 
   190 |     return () => unsub();
   191 |   }, []);
   192 | 
   193 |   const {
   194 |     teamALabel,
   195 |     teamBLabel,
   196 |     standbyLabel,
   197 |     teamAId,
   198 |     teamBId,
   199 |     standbyId,
   200 |     teamASnapshot,
   201 |     teamBSnapshot,
   202 |     standbySnapshot,
   203 |     matchNumber,
   204 |     events = [],
   205 |     finalSummary,
   206 |     isFinished,
   207 |   } = matchDoc || {};
   208 | 
   209 |   // ✅ Always compute from events live; only fall back to finalSummary
   210 |   const computedScores = useMemo(() => {
   211 |     if (!matchDoc) return { goalsA: 0, goalsB: 0 };
   212 | 
   213 |     if (events && events.length > 0) {
   214 |       let gA = 0;
   215 |       let gB = 0;
   216 |       for (const e of events) {
   217 |         if (e.type === "goal") {
   218 |           if (e.teamId === matchDoc.teamAId) gA += 1;
   219 |           if (e.teamId === matchDoc.teamBId) gB += 1;
   220 |         }
   221 |       }
   222 |       return { goalsA: gA, goalsB: gB };
   223 |     }
   224 | 
   225 |     // fallback for old finished matches with only finalSummary stored
   226 |     if (finalSummary && typeof finalSummary.goalsA === "number") {
   227 |       return {
   228 |         goalsA: finalSummary.goalsA,
   229 |         goalsB: finalSummary.goalsB,
   230 |       };
   231 |     }
   232 | 
   233 |     return { goalsA: 0, goalsB: 0 };
   234 |   }, [matchDoc, events, finalSummary]);
   235 | 
   236 |   const { goalsA, goalsB } = computedScores;
   237 | 
   238 |   const teamAForDisplay = teamASnapshot || {
   239 |     id: teamAId,
   240 |     label: teamALabel,
   241 |     name: teamALabel,
   242 |   };
   243 | 
   244 |   const teamBForDisplay = teamBSnapshot || {
   245 |     id: teamBId,
   246 |     label: teamBLabel,
   247 |     name: teamBLabel,
   248 |   };
   249 | 
   250 |   const standbyForDisplay = standbySnapshot || {
   251 |     id: standbyId,
   252 |     label: standbyLabel,
   253 |     name: standbyLabel,
   254 |   };
   255 | 
   256 |   // simple sorted copy by timeSeconds, just in case
   257 |   const sortedEvents = useMemo(() => {
   258 |     return [...events].sort(
   259 |       (a, b) => (a.timeSeconds || 0) - (b.timeSeconds || 0)
   260 |     );
   261 |   }, [events]);
   262 | 
   263 |   const goalEvents = useMemo(() => {
   264 |     return sortedEvents
   265 |       .filter((e) => e?.type === "goal")
   266 |       .slice()
   267 |       .reverse();
   268 |   }, [sortedEvents]);
   269 | 
   270 |   // 🔁 Local 1-second countdown for smoother timer
   271 |   useEffect(() => {
   272 |     if (!matchDoc) return;
   273 |     if (isFinished) return;
   274 |     if (
   275 |       localSecondsLeft == null ||
   276 |       !Number.isFinite(localSecondsLeft) ||
   277 |       localSecondsLeft <= 0
   278 |     ) {
   279 |       return;
   280 |     }
   281 | 
   282 |     const id = setInterval(() => {
   283 |       setLocalSecondsLeft((prev) => {
   284 |         if (prev == null) return prev;
   285 |         const next = prev - 1;
   286 |         return next >= 0 ? next : 0;
   287 |       });
   288 |     }, 1000);
   289 | 
   290 |     return () => clearInterval(id);
   291 |   }, [matchDoc, isFinished, localSecondsLeft]);
   292 | 
   293 |   const hasLiveTimer =
   294 |     localSecondsLeft != null && Number.isFinite(localSecondsLeft);
   295 | 
   296 |   const timerText = hasLiveTimer ? formatSeconds(localSecondsLeft) : "--:--";
   297 | 
   298 |   return (
   299 |     <div className="page live-page">
   300 |       <header className="header">
   301 |         <button
   302 |           className="secondary-btn"
   303 |           type="button"
   304 |           onClick={goBack}
   305 |           style={{ marginBottom: "0.75rem" }}
   306 |         >
   307 |           ← Back to Home
   308 |         </button>
   309 | 
   310 |         <h1>Spectator View</h1>
   311 |         {matchNumber ? (
   312 |           <p>Watching match #{matchNumber}</p>
   313 |         ) : (
   314 |           <p>Live score tracker</p>

   445 |                   })}
   446 |                 </ul>

   458 |                   No events logged yet.
   459 |                 </p>

   461 |                 <ul>
   462 |                   {sortedEvents.map((e) => {
   463 |                     const typeLabel =
   464 |                       e.type === "shibobo" ? "Shibobo" : "Goal";
   465 |                     const who =
   466 |                       e.scorer ||
   467 |                       e.player ||
   468 |                       e.playerName ||
   469 |                       "Unknown player";
   470 |                     const assist =
   471 |                       e.assist && e.assist !== ""
   472 |                         ? ` · Assist: ${e.assist}`
   473 |                         : "";
   474 | 
   475 |                     return (
   476 |                       <li key={e.id} className="event-item spectator-compact-event-item">
   477 |                         <span>
   478 |                           [{formatSeconds(e.timeSeconds)}]{" "}
   479 |                           <strong>{typeLabel}</strong> – {who}
   480 |                           {assist}
   481 |                         </span>
   482 |                       </li>
   483 |                     );
   484 |                   })}
   485 |                 </ul>
   486 |               )}
   487 |             </div>
   488 |           </>
   489 |         )}
   490 |       </section>
   491 |     </div>
   492 |   );
   493 | }

   196 | function normalizeHexColor(v) {
   197 |   const raw = String(v || "").trim().replace(/[^#a-fA-F0-9]/g, "");

   199 |   if (/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(raw)) return raw.toUpperCase();

   212 |   const g = parseInt(clean.slice(2, 4), 16);
   213 |   const b = parseInt(clean.slice(4, 6), 16);
   214 |   return `rgba(${r}, ${g}, ${b}, ${alpha})`;
   215 | }

   629 | function repairLeagueCurrentMatch(match, teams = [], fallbackActiveTeamIds = []) {
   630 |   const safeTeams = Array.isArray(teams) ? teams.filter((team) => team?.id) : [];
   631 |   const teamIds = safeTeams.map((team) => team.id);

   635 |       teamAId: teamIds[0] || null,
   636 |       teamBId: teamIds[1] || null,
   637 |       standbyId: teamIds[2] || null,
   638 |     };
   639 |   }

   642 |     new Set(
   643 |       (Array.isArray(fallbackActiveTeamIds) ? fallbackActiveTeamIds : []).filter(
   644 |         (teamId) => teamIds.includes(teamId)
   645 |       )
   646 |     )
   647 |   );

   650 |   const rawB = match?.teamBId || null;
   651 |   const rawStandby = match?.standbyId || null;
   652 | 
   653 |   const hasValidDistinctPair =
   654 |     rawA && rawB && rawA !== rawB && teamIds.includes(rawA) && teamIds.includes(rawB);
   655 |

   659 |       teamBId: rawB,
   660 |       standbyId:
   661 |         rawStandby &&
   662 |         teamIds.includes(rawStandby) &&
   663 |         rawStandby !== rawA &&
   664 |         rawStandby !== rawB
   665 |           ? rawStandby
   666 |           : teamIds.find((teamId) => teamId !== rawA && teamId !== rawB) || null,
   667 |     };
   668 |   }
   669 | 
   670 |   const teamAId = fallbackIds[0] || teamIds[0] || null;
   671 |   const teamBId =
   672 |     fallbackIds.find((teamId) => teamId !== teamAId) ||
   673 |     teamIds.find((teamId) => teamId !== teamAId) ||
   674 |     null;
   675 | 
   676 |   return {
   677 |     teamAId,
   678 |     teamBId,
   679 |     standbyId: teamIds.find((teamId) => teamId !== teamAId && teamId !== teamBId) || null,
   680 |   };
   681 | }

   686 | function ensureSeasonSchedulingShape(season) {
   687 |   if (!season || typeof season !== "object") return season;
   688 | 
   689 |   const teamIds = Array.isArray(season?.teams)
   690 |     ? season.teams.map((team) => team?.id).filter(Boolean)

   692 | 
   693 |   const rawActiveTeamIds = Array.isArray(season?.activeTeamIds)

   695 |     : [];

   698 |     new Set(
   699 |       (rawActiveTeamIds.length ? rawActiveTeamIds : teamIds.slice(0, 2)).filter(
   700 |         (teamId) => teamIds.includes(teamId)
   701 |       )
   702 |     )
   703 |   ).slice(0, 2);
   704 | 
   705 |   const legacyGameFormat = season?.gameFormat || GAME_FORMAT.FIVE_V_FIVE;
   706 |   const resolvedMatchType = normalizeMatchMode(
   707 |     season?.matchType || legacyGameFormat,
   708 |     MATCH_TYPE.FRIENDLY
   709 |   );
   710 |   const resolvedGameFormat = normalizeGameFormat(
   711 |     legacyGameFormat,
   712 |     GAME_FORMAT.FIVE_V_FIVE
   713 |   );
   714 | 
   715 |   return {
   716 |     ...season,
   717 |     matchType: resolvedMatchType,
   718 |     gameFormat: resolvedGameFormat,
   719 |     activeTeamIds:
   720 |       normalizedActiveTeamIds.length >= 2
   721 |         ? normalizedActiveTeamIds
   722 |         : teamIds.slice(0, 2),
   723 |     fiveVFiveTeams:
   724 |       season?.isPracticeSeason || String(season?.seasonId || "").startsWith("practice")
   725 |         ? normalizeFiveVFiveTeams(season?.fiveVFiveTeams)
   726 |         : ensureFiveVFiveTeamsShape(season?.fiveVFiveTeams),
   727 |     matchMode: season?.matchMode || "round_robin",

  1873 | function secondsLeftFromExpectedEnd(expectedEndAtISO) {
  1874 |   const endMs = new Date(expectedEndAtISO || "").getTime();
  1875 |   if (!Number.isFinite(endMs)) return null;
  1876 |   return Math.max(0, Math.ceil((endMs - Date.now()) / 1000));

  1879 | function addSecondsToISO(startISO, seconds) {

  1881 |   const safeStart = Number.isFinite(startMs) ? startMs : Date.now();
  1882 |   return new Date(safeStart + Math.max(0, Number(seconds || 0)) * 1000).toISOString();

  1888 | 
  1889 |   const rounded = new Date(date);
  1890 |   rounded.setSeconds(0, 0);
  1891 |   rounded.setMinutes(Math.round(rounded.getMinutes() / 15) * 15);

  1913 | function buildClubScheduledFinishISO(playTime, startISO, fallbackSeconds) {
  1914 |   const start = new Date(startISO || Date.now());
  1915 | 
  1916 |   if (!Number.isFinite(start.getTime())) {
  1917 |     return addSecondsToISO(startISO, fallbackSeconds);
  1918 |   }
  1919 | 
  1920 |   const officialFinishISO = buildClubOfficialScheduledFinishISO(
  1921 |     playTime,
  1922 |     startISO
  1923 |   );
  1924 | 
  1925 |   if (!officialFinishISO) {
  1926 |     const recommended = roundDateToNearestQuarterHour(
  1927 |       new Date(start.getTime() + Math.max(0, Number(fallbackSeconds || 3600)) * 1000)
  1928 |     );
  1929 | 
  1930 |     return recommended?.toISOString() || addSecondsToISO(startISO, fallbackSeconds);
  1931 |   }
  1932 | 
  1933 |   const officialFinish = new Date(officialFinishISO);
  1934 |   const scheduledStart = new Date(officialFinish);
  1935 |   scheduledStart.setMinutes(scheduledStart.getMinutes() - 60);
  1936 | 
  1937 |   // Treat kickoff as part of the scheduled session when it begins
  1938 |   // no more than 30 minutes before the scheduled start and no more
  1939 |   // than 30 minutes after it.
  1940 |   const earliestScheduledKickoff = scheduledStart.getTime() - 30 * 60 * 1000;
  1941 |   const latestScheduledKickoff = scheduledStart.getTime() + 30 * 60 * 1000;
  1942 |   const kickoffIsNearSchedule =
  1943 |     start.getTime() >= earliestScheduledKickoff &&
  1944 |     start.getTime() <= latestScheduledKickoff;
  1945 | 
  1946 |   if (kickoffIsNearSchedule && officialFinish.getTime() > start.getTime()) {
  1947 |     return officialFinish.toISOString();
  1948 |   }
  1949 | 
  1950 |   const recommended = roundDateToNearestQuarterHour(
  1951 |     new Date(start.getTime() + 60 * 60 * 1000)
  1952 |   );
  1953 | 
  1954 |   return recommended?.toISOString() || addSecondsToISO(startISO, fallbackSeconds);
  1955 | }

  2030 |     saturday: 6, saturdays: 6,
  2031 |   };
  2032 | 
  2033 |   const dayKey = Object.keys(dayMap).find((key) => raw.includes(key));
  2034 |   const timeMatch = raw.match(/(\d{1,2})[:h](\d{2})(?:\s*[–-]\s*(\d{1,2})[:h](\d{2}))?/);
  2035 | 
  2036 |   if (!dayKey || !timeMatch) return null;
  2037 | 
  2038 |   const [, sh, sm, rawEh, rawEm] = timeMatch;
  2039 |   const eh = rawEh || String(Number(sh) + 2);
  2040 |   const em = rawEm || sm;
  2041 |   return {
  2042 |     day: dayMap[dayKey],
  2043 |     startMinutes: Number(sh) * 60 + Number(sm),
  2044 |     endMinutes: Number(eh) * 60 + Number(em),
  2045 |   };
  2046 | }
  2047 | 
  2048 | function isInsideClubWeeklyWindow(weeklyPlayTime, now = new Date()) {
  2049 |   const windowInfo = parseClubWeeklyWindow(weeklyPlayTime);
  2050 |   if (!windowInfo) return false;
  2051 | 
  2052 |   const currentDay = now.getDay();
  2053 |   const currentMinutes = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
  2054 | 
  2055 |   // 30-minute grace before and after the official window.
  2056 |   return (
  2057 |     currentDay === windowInfo.day &&
  2058 |     currentMinutes >= windowInfo.startMinutes - 30 &&
  2059 |     currentMinutes <= windowInfo.endMinutes + 30
  2060 |   );
  2061 | }

  3226 |       if (result?.teamAId && counts[result.teamAId] != null) {

  3228 |       }
  3229 |       if (result?.teamBId && counts[result.teamBId] != null) {
  3230 |         counts[result.teamBId] += 1;
  3231 |       }
  3232 |     });
  3233 | 
  3234 |     return counts;
  3235 |   }, [teams, fullResults]);
  3236 | 
  3237 |   const currentMaxP = useMemo(() => {

  4654 |   const handleDeleteSavedMatch = (matchNoToDelete, deleteContext = {}) => {
  4655 |     if (!USE_V2) return;
  4656 | 
  4657 |     const requestedMatchType = normalizeMatchMode(
  4658 |       deleteContext?.matchType || matchType,
  4659 |       MATCH_TYPE.LEAGUE
  4660 |     );
  4661 |     const requestedMatchDayId = String(deleteContext?.matchDayId || "").trim();
  4662 | 
  4663 |     updateActiveSeason((prevSeason) => {
  4664 |       if (requestedMatchType === MATCH_TYPE.FRIENDLY) {
  4665 |         const safeFriendlyHistory = Array.isArray(prevSeason?.friendlyMatchDayHistory)
  4666 |           ? prevSeason.friendlyMatchDayHistory
  4667 |           : [];
  4668 | 
  4669 |         const nextFriendlyHistory = safeFriendlyHistory
  4670 |           .map((day) => {
  4671 |             const dayId = String(day?.id || day?.matchDayId || day?.date || "").trim();
  4672 | 
  4673 |             // Preferred path: one Friendly record represents one Friendly day/match.
  4674 |             // Remove the whole archived friendly entry when the IDs match.
  4675 |             if (requestedMatchDayId && dayId && dayId === requestedMatchDayId) {
  4676 |               return null;
  4677 |             }
  4678 | 
  4679 |             const dayResults = Array.isArray(day?.results) ? day.results : [];
  4680 |             const dayEvents = Array.isArray(day?.allEvents) ? day.allEvents : [];
  4681 | 
  4682 |             const nextResults = dayResults.filter(
  4683 |               (r) => Number(r?.matchNo) !== Number(matchNoToDelete)
  4684 |             );
  4685 |             const nextEvents = dayEvents.filter(
  4686 |               (e) => Number(e?.matchNo) !== Number(matchNoToDelete)
  4687 |             );
  4688 | 
  4689 |             if (nextResults.length === 0 && nextEvents.length === 0) {
  4690 |               return null;
  4691 |             }
  4692 | 
  4693 |             return {
  4694 |               ...day,
  4695 |               results: nextResults,
  4696 |               allEvents: nextEvents,
  4697 |             };
  4698 |           })
  4699 |           .filter(Boolean);
  4700 | 
  4701 |         return {
  4702 |           ...prevSeason,
  4703 |           friendlyMatchDayHistory: nextFriendlyHistory,
  4704 |         };
  4705 |       }
  4706 | 
  4707 |       const safeResults = Array.isArray(prevSeason?.results)
  4708 |         ? prevSeason.results
  4709 |         : [];
  4710 |       const safeAllEvents = Array.isArray(prevSeason?.allEvents)
  4711 |         ? prevSeason.allEvents
  4712 |         : [];
  4713 | 
  4714 |       return {
  4715 |         ...prevSeason,
  4716 |         results: safeResults.filter(
  4717 |           (r) => Number(r?.matchNo) !== Number(matchNoToDelete)
  4718 |         ),
  4719 |         allEvents: safeAllEvents.filter(
  4720 |           (e) => Number(e?.matchNo) !== Number(matchNoToDelete)
  4721 |         ),
  4722 |       };
  4723 |     });
  4724 |   };

  5037 |   const handleParticipationChange = (entryKey, rawValue) => {
  5038 |     const numeric = Number(rawValue);
  5039 | 
  5040 |     setPendingParticipationEntries((prev) =>
  5041 |       prev.map((entry) => {
  5042 |         if (entry.key !== entryKey) return entry;
  5043 | 
  5044 |         const capped = Math.max(
  5045 |           0,
  5046 |           Math.min(
  5047 |             Number(entry.teamMatches || 0),
  5048 |             Number.isFinite(numeric) ? numeric : 0
  5049 |           )
  5050 |         );
  5051 | 
  5052 |         return {
  5053 |           ...entry,
  5054 |           matchesPlayed: capped,
  5055 |         };
  5056 |       })
  5057 |     );
  5058 |   };
  5059 | 
  5060 |   const handleParticipationStep = (entryKey, delta) => {
  5061 |     setPendingParticipationEntries((prev) =>
  5062 |       prev.map((entry) => {
  5063 |         if (entry.key !== entryKey) return entry;
  5064 |         const nextValue = Number(entry.matchesPlayed || 0) + Number(delta || 0);
  5065 |         const capped = Math.max(
  5066 |           0,
  5067 |           Math.min(Number(entry.teamMatches || 0), nextValue)
  5068 |         );
  5069 |         return {
  5070 |           ...entry,
  5071 |           matchesPlayed: capped,
  5072 |         };
  5073 |       })
  5074 |     );
  5075 |   };

   105 | function buildClubStorageKey(name, clubId = DEFAULT_CLUB_ID) {
   106 |   const safeName = String(name || "value").trim().replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "_");
   107 |   const safeClubId = String(clubId || DEFAULT_CLUB_ID)
   108 |     .trim()
   109 |     .toLowerCase()
   110 |     .replace(/\s+/g, "-")
   111 |     .replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/g, "") || DEFAULT_CLUB_ID;
   112 | 
   113 |   return `fanm_${safeClubId}_${safeName}`;
   114 | }
   115 | 
   116 | function getIdentityStorageKey(clubId = DEFAULT_CLUB_ID) {
   117 |   return buildClubStorageKey("identity_v1", clubId);
   118 | }
   119 | 
   120 | function getRefereeDeviceStorageKey(clubId = DEFAULT_CLUB_ID) {
   121 |   return buildClubStorageKey("referee_device_id_v1", clubId);
   122 | }
   123 | 
   124 | function getOrCreateRefereeDeviceId(clubId = DEFAULT_CLUB_ID) {
   125 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return "server-device";
   126 | 
   127 |   const key = getRefereeDeviceStorageKey(clubId);
   128 | 
   129 |   try {
   130 |     const existing = window.localStorage.getItem(key);
   131 | 
   132 |     if (existing) return existing;
   133 | 
   134 |     const created =
   135 |       `device-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`;
   136 | 
   137 |     window.localStorage.setItem(key, created);
   138 | 
   139 |     return created;
   140 |   } catch {
   141 |     return `device-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`;
   142 |   }
   143 | }
   144 |

   150 |   return buildClubStorageKey(MATCH_SECONDS_STORAGE_NAME, clubId);
   151 | }
   152 | 
   153 | function getPlayerCardSnapshotStorageKeys(seasonId, clubId = DEFAULT_CLUB_ID) {
   154 |   const safeClubId = String(clubId || DEFAULT_CLUB_ID).trim() || DEFAULT_CLUB_ID;
   155 |   const safeSeasonId = String(seasonId || "").trim();
   156 |

   159 |         buildClubStorageKey(`player_card_snapshot_${safeSeasonId}`, safeClubId),

   166 |     keys.push("tk_player_card_snapshot_latest");

  1009 | function computeExpectedFullMatches(teamMatches, squadSize) {
  1010 |   const matches = Number(teamMatches || 0);
  1011 |   const size = Number(squadSize || 0);
  1012 | 
  1013 |   if (matches <= 0) return 0;
  1014 |   if (size <= 5) return matches;
  1015 | 
  1016 |   return Math.round((matches * 5) / size);
  1017 | }

  1065 | function readRenderedPlayerCardSnapshotFromLocalStorage(seasonId, clubId = DEFAULT_CLUB_ID) {

  1873 | function secondsLeftFromExpectedEnd(expectedEndAtISO) {

  1879 | function addSecondsToISO(startISO, seconds) {

  1881 |   const safeStart = Number.isFinite(startMs) ? startMs : Date.now();
  1882 |   return new Date(safeStart + Math.max(0, Number(seconds || 0)) * 1000).toISOString();

  1885 | function roundDateToNearestQuarterHour(value) {

  1888 | 
  1889 |   const rounded = new Date(date);

  1891 |   rounded.setMinutes(Math.round(rounded.getMinutes() / 15) * 15);
  1892 | 
  1893 |   return rounded;

  1913 | function buildClubScheduledFinishISO(playTime, startISO, fallbackSeconds) {

  2019 | function parseClubWeeklyWindow(text) {
  2020 |   const raw = String(text || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  2021 |   if (!raw) return null;
  2022 | 
  2023 |   const dayMap = {
  2024 |     sunday: 0, sundays: 0,
  2025 |     monday: 1, mondays: 1,
  2026 |     tuesday: 2, tuesdays: 2,
  2027 |     wednesday: 3, wednesdays: 3,
  2028 |     thursday: 4, thursdays: 4,
  2029 |     friday: 5, fridays: 5,
  2030 |     saturday: 6, saturdays: 6,
  2031 |   };
  2032 | 
  2033 |   const dayKey = Object.keys(dayMap).find((key) => raw.includes(key));
  2034 |   const timeMatch = raw.match(/(\d{1,2})[:h](\d{2})(?:\s*[–-]\s*(\d{1,2})[:h](\d{2}))?/);
  2035 | 
  2036 |   if (!dayKey || !timeMatch) return null;
  2037 | 
  2038 |   const [, sh, sm, rawEh, rawEm] = timeMatch;
  2039 |   const eh = rawEh || String(Number(sh) + 2);
  2040 |   const em = rawEm || sm;
  2041 |   return {
  2042 |     day: dayMap[dayKey],
  2043 |     startMinutes: Number(sh) * 60 + Number(sm),
  2044 |     endMinutes: Number(eh) * 60 + Number(em),
  2045 |   };
  2046 | }
  2047 | 
  2048 | function isInsideClubWeeklyWindow(weeklyPlayTime, now = new Date()) {
  2049 |   const windowInfo = parseClubWeeklyWindow(weeklyPlayTime);
  2050 |   if (!windowInfo) return false;
  2051 | 
  2052 |   const currentDay = now.getDay();
  2053 |   const currentMinutes = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
  2054 | 
  2055 |   // 30-minute grace before and after the official window.
  2056 |   return (
  2057 |     currentDay === windowInfo.day &&
  2058 |     currentMinutes >= windowInfo.startMinutes - 30 &&
  2059 |     currentMinutes <= windowInfo.endMinutes + 30
  2060 |   );
  2061 | }

  4653 | 
  4654 |   const handleDeleteSavedMatch = (matchNoToDelete, deleteContext = {}) => {
  4655 |     if (!USE_V2) return;
  4656 | 
  4657 |     const requestedMatchType = normalizeMatchMode(
  4658 |       deleteContext?.matchType || matchType,
  4659 |       MATCH_TYPE.LEAGUE
  4660 |     );
  4661 |     const requestedMatchDayId = String(deleteContext?.matchDayId || "").trim();
  4662 | 
  4663 |     updateActiveSeason((prevSeason) => {
  4664 |       if (requestedMatchType === MATCH_TYPE.FRIENDLY) {
  4665 |         const safeFriendlyHistory = Array.isArray(prevSeason?.friendlyMatchDayHistory)
  4666 |           ? prevSeason.friendlyMatchDayHistory
  4667 |           : [];
  4668 | 
  4669 |         const nextFriendlyHistory = safeFriendlyHistory
  4670 |           .map((day) => {
  4671 |             const dayId = String(day?.id || day?.matchDayId || day?.date || "").trim();
  4672 | 
  4673 |             // Preferred path: one Friendly record represents one Friendly day/match.
  4674 |             // Remove the whole archived friendly entry when the IDs match.
  4675 |             if (requestedMatchDayId && dayId && dayId === requestedMatchDayId) {
  4676 |               return null;
  4677 |             }
  4678 | 
  4679 |             const dayResults = Array.isArray(day?.results) ? day.results : [];
  4680 |             const dayEvents = Array.isArray(day?.allEvents) ? day.allEvents : [];
  4681 | 
  4682 |             const nextResults = dayResults.filter(
  4683 |               (r) => Number(r?.matchNo) !== Number(matchNoToDelete)
  4684 |             );
  4685 |             const nextEvents = dayEvents.filter(
  4686 |               (e) => Number(e?.matchNo) !== Number(matchNoToDelete)
  4687 |             );
  4688 | 
  4689 |             if (nextResults.length === 0 && nextEvents.length === 0) {
  4690 |               return null;
  4691 |             }
  4692 | 
  4693 |             return {
  4694 |               ...day,
  4695 |               results: nextResults,
  4696 |               allEvents: nextEvents,
  4697 |             };
  4698 |           })
  4699 |           .filter(Boolean);
  4700 | 
  4701 |         return {
  4702 |           ...prevSeason,
  4703 |           friendlyMatchDayHistory: nextFriendlyHistory,
  4704 |         };
  4705 |       }
  4706 | 
  4707 |       const safeResults = Array.isArray(prevSeason?.results)
  4708 |         ? prevSeason.results
  4709 |         : [];
  4710 |       const safeAllEvents = Array.isArray(prevSeason?.allEvents)
  4711 |         ? prevSeason.allEvents
  4712 |         : [];
  4713 | 
  4714 |       return {
  4715 |         ...prevSeason,
  4716 |         results: safeResults.filter(
  4717 |           (r) => Number(r?.matchNo) !== Number(matchNoToDelete)
  4718 |         ),
  4719 |         allEvents: safeAllEvents.filter(
  4720 |           (e) => Number(e?.matchNo) !== Number(matchNoToDelete)
  4721 |         ),
  4722 |       };
  4723 |     });
  4724 |   };

  5037 |   const handleParticipationChange = (entryKey, rawValue) => {
  5038 |     const numeric = Number(rawValue);
  5039 | 
  5040 |     setPendingParticipationEntries((prev) =>
  5041 |       prev.map((entry) => {
  5042 |         if (entry.key !== entryKey) return entry;
  5043 | 
  5044 |         const capped = Math.max(
  5045 |           0,
  5046 |           Math.min(
  5047 |             Number(entry.teamMatches || 0),
  5048 |             Number.isFinite(numeric) ? numeric : 0
  5049 |           )
  5050 |         );
  5051 | 
  5052 |         return {
  5053 |           ...entry,
  5054 |           matchesPlayed: capped,
  5055 |         };
  5056 |       })
  5057 |     );
  5058 |   };

  5061 |     setPendingParticipationEntries((prev) =>
  5062 |       prev.map((entry) => {
  5063 |         if (entry.key !== entryKey) return entry;
  5064 |         const nextValue = Number(entry.matchesPlayed || 0) + Number(delta || 0);
  5065 |         const capped = Math.max(
  5066 |           0,
  5067 |           Math.min(Number(entry.teamMatches || 0), nextValue)
  5068 |         );
  5069 |         return {
  5070 |           ...entry,
  5071 |           matchesPlayed: capped,
  5072 |         };
  5073 |       })
  5074 |     );

  1982 |     const kickoffText = String(fixture?.proposedKickoff || "23:59").trim();
  1983 |     const timeText = /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(kickoffText) ? kickoffText : "23:59";
  1984 |     const fixtureTime = new Date(`${dateText}T${timeText}:00`);
  1985 | 
  1986 |     if (Number.isNaN(fixtureTime.getTime())) return true;
  1987 | 
  1988 |     return fixtureTime.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 >= Date.now();

   832 |               <section className="challenge-ops-actions">
   833 |                 <article className="challenge-ops-action challenge-ops-action--start">
   834 |                   <span className="challenge-ops-action__icon">▶</span>
   835 |                   <div>
   836 |                     <small>Official controls</small>
   837 |                     <h2>
   838 |                       {fixtureLive
   839 |                         ? "Match in progress"
   840 |                         : fixtureComplete
   841 |                           ? "Match completed"
   842 |                           : "Start Match"}
   843 |                     </h2>
   844 |                     <p>
   845 |                       Launch the club-versus-club referee tool based on
   846 |                       the Friendly Live Match Page.
   847 |                     </p>
   848 |                   </div>
   849 |                   <button
   850 |                     type="button"
   851 |                     disabled={!fixtureReady || fixtureComplete}
   852 |                     onClick={() =>
   853 |                       onOpenMatchControl?.(selectedFixture)
   854 |                     }
   855 |                   >
   856 |                     {fixtureLive
   857 |                       ? "Resume Match"
   858 |                       : fixtureComplete
   859 |                         ? "Full Time"
   860 |                         : "Start Match"}
   861 |                     <span>→</span>
   862 |                   </button>
   863 |                 </article>
   864 | 
   865 |                 <article className="challenge-ops-action challenge-ops-action--report">
   866 |                   <span className="challenge-ops-action__icon">◉</span>
   867 |                   <div>
   868 |                     <small>Live spectator feed</small>
   869 |                     <h2>Match Report</h2>
   870 |                     <p>
   871 |                       View the live score, goals, cards, injuries and
   872 |                       final match report.
   873 |                     </p>
   874 |                   </div>
   875 | 
   876 |                   <div className="challenge-ops-action__score">
   877 |                     <strong>{homeScore}</strong>
   878 |                     <span>–</span>
   879 |                     <strong>{awayScore}</strong>
   880 |                   </div>
   881 | 
   882 |                   <button
   883 |                     type="button"
   884 |                     onClick={() =>
   885 |                       onOpenMatchReport?.(selectedFixture)
   886 |                     }
   887 |                   >
   888 |                     {fixtureComplete
   889 |                       ? "View Final Report"
   890 |                       : fixtureLive
   891 |                         ? "View Live Report"
   892 |                         : "Open Match Report"}
   893 |                     <span>→</span>
   894 |                   </button>
   895 |                 </article>
   896 |               </section>
   897 |             </>
   898 |           )}
   899 |         </section>
   900 |       </div>
   901 |     </main>
   902 |   );
   903 | }
